# Graph Report - linorobot2  (2026-07-20)

## Corpus Check
- 4942 files · ~4,536,846 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 27671 nodes · 71029 edges · 1609 communities (1123 shown, 486 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 85% EXTRACTED · 15% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 10414 edges (avg confidence: 0.8)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `a267f959`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
